## Corpus Check
- 4879 files · ~5,844,786 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 30 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 13, .css 4, .csv 3)

## Summary
- 54804 nodes · 173784 edges · 1352 communities (1095 shown, 257 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 21242 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `677f8816`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).

## Import Cycles
- 3-file cycle: `apps/api/app/services/partnership_tasks.py -> apps/api/app/services/university_visits.py -> apps/api/app/services/partnership_universities.py -> apps/api/app/services/partnership_tasks.py`
